# ICOT archive outside `ifs/`: software, contest sources and software pages, local mirror manifest

Retrieved 2026-11-20 from the AIST-hosted ICOT archive. Companion to `IFS_MANIFEST.md` (the `ifs/` tree and `Museum/IFS/`).
Everything below is held as data only: nothing was unpacked into the repository, built or run.

- Files: `https://www.airc.aist.go.jp/aitec-icot/ICOT/<path>` -> `icot-other/<path>`.
- Census: AIST serves no directory indexes, so the whole archive root was walked afresh on the second mirror,
  `https://www.uedalab.jp/AITEC_ICOT_ARCHIVES/ICOT/`, skipping `ifs/` (held, `IFS_MANIFEST.md`) and `Museum/TRTM/` (technical reports, held elsewhere under `lit/`):
  888 directories listed, 15,972 files. Every file here was then fetched from AIST, none from the second mirror.
- Six directories serve an index page instead of a listing. Two are in scope (`Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/`, `pub/contest/1996/SRC/FREE/New/KASAKU/readme.dir/`)
  and were enumerated by following links from their `index.html` and by walking their listable subdirectories; a file in `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/` itself or
  `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/` itself that no page links cannot be seen and would be missing. The other four are `Museum/InvitedResearcher/*/` (not software, not fetched).
- Checksums: `icot-other/SHA256SUMS` (paths relative to `icot-other/`; check with `cd icot-other && shasum -a 256 -c SHA256SUMS`).

## 1. Scope

| Taken | What |
|---|---|
| `pub/` | everything: KL1 programming contest sources 1996-98 (`pub/contest/`), `pub/klic/`, `pub/cuprolog/`, `pub/m-qxt/` and the rest |
| `Museum/SOFTWARE/` | everything: KAPPA, QUIXOTE (including `QUIXOTE/HomePage-E.html`, linked from IFS abstracts 79 and 80), MGTP, GIP, LEGAL pages |
| `Museum/FGCS94/` | everything, including the 15 `PS/*.ps.gz` papers |
| `Museum/FinalEval/GIF/tm1216.tgz` | the one archive file in `Museum/FinalEval/` |
| `Free-Software/` | the five software entry pages |

Not taken: the remaining 14,000-odd files of the archive, which the census shows to be HTML, GIF/JPEG/PNG, PDF, video and audio (minutes, funding, demo, history, proceedings and report pages).
Outside the directories above and `ifs/`, the census found no compressed archive and no program source file; the only `.pl` files there are LaTeX2HTML `images.pl`/`labels.pl` page-generation leftovers.

| `Free-Software` | 5 | 30773 |
| `Museum/FGCS94` | 88 | 2329914 |

By type (first bytes of the file; "tar" where `tar tf` lists it):

| Type | Files |

| DVI | 2 |
| GIF | 502 |
| HTML | 660 |
| PostScript | 2 |
| binary | 27 |
| compress tar | 2 |
| gzip | 15 |
| gzip tar | 47 |
| tar | 1 |
| text | 226 |

Archives listed: 50 (35736060 bytes), all without error. Largest file: `icot-other/pub/cuprolog/demo.tar.Z`, 21330677 bytes. No file is over 95 MB.

### Checks made on every fetched file

HTTP 200 on the GET; local size equal to the Content-Length of a separate HEAD; first bytes agree with the extension
(`1f 9d` for `.Z`, `1f 8b` for `.gz`/`.tgz`, `%!PS` for `.ps`, `%PDF` for `.pdf`); not an HTML page under a non-HTML name; not the 5995-byte AIST 404 body;
every archive listed with `tar tf` and the entry count recorded. After copying into the repository each file was re-hashed and compared with the staged copy. Exceptions are in section 4.

## 3. Fetched files

## 4. Failed, skipped, and notes

- **Failed (10):** linked from a page but HTTP 500 on AIST (its answer for a missing file) and absent from the second mirror's listing (404 there for the one tried): `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img1.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img10.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img2.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img3.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img4.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img5.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img6.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img7.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img8.gif`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/exercise/img9.gif`. These are dead image links in the MGTP lecture pages, not files that exist.
- **Skipped:** nothing in scope. The census total was about 54 MB, under the 1 GB limit.
- **Magic differs from extension (kept as served):** `icot-other/Museum/FinalEval/GIF/tm1216.tgz` is an uncompressed tar (161 entries) despite the `.tgz` name.
- **HTML under a non-HTML name (kept as served):** `icot-other/Museum/SOFTWARE/QUIXOTE/slides/amateurJ/buttonslide1.ori` and `.../beginnerJ/onlyslide1.old` are backup copies of HTML pages, not error pages.
- **No Content-Length on HEAD (15 files;** size check not possible, HTTP 200 confirmed and a second GET gave the same SHA-256): `Museum/FGCS94/Chikayama/chikayama-proc.html`, `Museum/FGCS94/general-report-J.html`, `Museum/FGCS94/Nitta/process.gif`, `Museum/FGCS94/uchida-proc.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/GIP/gene_alignment.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/GIP/gene_links.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/LEGAL/hellic-II.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/LEGAL/ronri-j.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/gateways`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/hosts`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/html/sample-mgtp.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/MGTP/lecture/networks`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/QUIXOTE/example/music.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/QUIXOTE/example/sumo.html`, `Museum/SOFTWARE/QUIXOTE/intro/name.html`. Four of them are empty (0 bytes) on the server.
